segment.start === 'number' ? segment.start : 0;\n      const safeEnd = typeof segment.end === 'number' ? segment.end : 0;\n      // 确保start ≤ end（修复时间倒流）\n      const validStart = Math.min(safeStart, safeEnd);\n      const validEnd = Math.max(safeStart, safeEnd);\n      return {\n        start: validStart + timeOffset,\n        end: validEnd + timeOffset\n      };\n    });\n  \n    // Math.min(...allStarts) : 0;\n    const lastEnd = allEnds.length > 0 ? Math.max(...allEnds) : 0;\n    const duration = lastEnd - firstStart;\n  \n    // 总偏移区间\n    const time = [{ \n      start: timeOffset, \n      end: timeOffset + duration \n    }];\n  \n    // * 支持4个时间输入的合并代码\n * 功能：接收4个时间数组（time1-time4），提取第一个时间对象，验证后合并\n */\n\n// 单个时间片段对象结构\ninterface TimeSegment {\n    start: any;\n    end: any;\n}\n\n// 输入参数结构（含4个时间数组）\ninterface InputParams {\n    params: {\n        time1: TimeSegment[]; // 数组类型，含时间对象\n        time2: TimeSegment[];\n        time3: TimeSegment[];\n        time4: TimeSegment[]; // 新增第四个时间数组\n        mergeOverlaps?: boolean; //
